```

export function resolveDatabasePath(moduleUrl: string | URL = import.meta.url, envPath = process.env.DATABASE_PATH): string {
  const repoRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../", moduleUrl));
  if (envPath) return isAbsolute(envPath) ? envPath : resolve(repoRoot, envPath);
  return resolve(repoRoot, ".data", "link-lab.db");
}
```

Define `buildExplanation(result: RunResult, input: RunInput): string` using pathResults only (already server-generated) + alertCount:
- Map A/B.
- If both blocked:
  `两条已建模路径均未到达模拟数据：路径 A 在 ${firstBlockedStepId} 被阻断（${blockedReason}）；路径 B 在 ${firstBlockedStepId} 被阻断（${blockedReason}）。`
- If A reached, B blocked:
  `路径 B 在 ${step} 被阻断（${reason}），但路径 A 仍到达模拟数据。`
- If A blocked, B reached:
  `路径 A 在 ${step} 被阻断（${reason}），但路径 B 仍到达模拟数据。`
- Both reached: `两条已建模路径均到达模拟数据，当前配置未阻断任何已建模路径。`
- Alert clause appended before final period: if alertCount>0 `；D3 记录了 ${alertCount} 条敏感请求告警，但未自动阻断。` else `。`

Now "旧请求返回时如何避免把保存成功错挂到新的配置上" — detailed sequence:
- Each displayed successful run identified by `runEpoch` (already increments on every new result, preset switch, scenario reload) and by its input config key.
- Save handler captures `{ epochAtClick, configKeyAtClick, token }` from the displayed `runState`.
- Immediately `setSaveState(saving)`.
- On response, these must all hold: `token === saveTokenRef.current`, `epochAtClick === current runEpoch` (passed via a ref mirror? Need careful because in async closure, comparing to current state variable captured at handler creation would be stale. We need a ref mirror `runEpochRef` updated synchronously whenever runEpoch changes. Currently `runEpoch` state changes via `setRunEpoch`; could add helper `bumpRunEpoch()` that increments ref and state. Or compare to `runVersionRef.current`. `runVersionRef` increments in `run()` start and preset switch; but `loadScenario` increments runEpoch, not runVersion. Scenario reload also resets scenario; we'd reset save token there. For result-change identity, runVersion is incremented at run start, so a save from previous done would be stale as soon as a new run starts. But scenario reload does not increment runVersion; we explicitly invalidate save in loadScenario and preset switch. And a new result can only arrive from a run whose start incremented runVersion. So `runVersionRef.current` is a valid ref gate for "no new run started". However if save starts after a done run, then user clicks manual run (runVersion++), old save resolves: runVersion mismatch → discard. Good. If scenario reloads (runVersion not bumped), we explicitly bump saveToken and reset saveState; response token mismatch. Good. If preset switch (runVersion++), discard. 
- Also `configKey` compare at render/response ensures the status is only tied to that displayed input. Since runVersion changes on any new run, config key may be redundant, but add `configKey` for render guard.
- On response, require `runStateRef.current.status === "done"` maybe. runVersion gate enough.
- If discarded, silently return; no "saved" state; no new error toast.
- `finally` only clear `saving` if token still current; otherwise don't touch new state.
- While saving, disable save button; but other controls (defense/slider) still work; new run is allowed intentionally (not auto-save). The save state resets to idle at run start. This means the in-flight save may still succeed server-side even though UI discards status; that's acceptable? Requirement says avoid misattaching; record was created for old config. Could abort fetch on new run with AbortController so no orphan record? Hmm, user says "旧请求返回时如何避免把'保存成功'错挂到新的配置上" — they care about UI status, not preventing record. But we could use AbortController to cancel save when run changes. However then server may still have saved. Not needed. Plan: discard UI result only; record may exist for the old config, and that's correct because user did click save for it. Perhaps mention.
- Save status cleared on run start / preset switch / scenario reload, not on playback index changes (playback is same run). Good nuance: playing events must not clear saved status.
- Also no auto-save from `run()`; only explicit button.

Potential UI state: `saveState` saved must persist across playback (same run). Yes.

App close: `onClose` hook. `server.ts`:
```
const app = buildServer();

process.once("SIGINT", () => void shutdown());
process.once("SIGTERM", () => void shutdown());
```

我已读完指定文件。当前 `/api` 只有健康检查、固定场景与 `/api/evaluations`，`buildServer` 不持有数据库；前端已有 `runVersionRef`、`runEpoch` 两条“旧响应作废”闸门，但没有保存入口。以下只给计划，未修改任何文件、未运行任何修改命令。

## 总体数据流

```text
工作台当前 done 运行
  → 点击“保存这次运行”
  → POST /api/runs（只发 RunInput，不发 result）
  → 服务器复用 runInputSchema 校验
  → evaluateRun(scenario, input) 重算 RunResult
  → buildRunExplanation(result) 生成一句模板解释
  → 单条 INSERT 写入 SQLite
  → 201 + RunRecord
GET /api/runs/:id
  → 单条 SELECT
  → 原样返回库内 input/result/scenarioVersion/explanation
```

## 1. `POST /api/runs` 与 `GET /api/runs/:id`

### `POST /api/runs`

请求体与 `/api/evaluations` 完全一致，直接复用现有 `runInputSchema`，不新增手写校验：

```json
{
  "scenarioId": "campus-registration",
  "presetId": "A",
  "conditions": { "O": "open", "T": "granted" },
  "defenses": ["D1", "D3"]
}
```

- `presetId` 仍只作为快照记录，不参与推演。
- 请求体不允许携带 `result`、`explanation` 等服务端结论字段，`additionalProperties: false` 会直接返回 400。
- 成功返回 **201**，响应类型统一为 `RunRecord`：

```json
{
  "id": "生成的 UUID",
  "input": { "scenarioId": "campus-registration", "presetId": "A", "conditions": { "O": "open", "T": "granted" }, "defenses": ["D1", "D3"] },
  "result": { "events": [...8 条...], "pathResults": [...], "alertCount": 2 },
  "scenarioVersion": 1,
  "explanation": "……一句规则模板解释……",
  "explanationVersion": 1,
  "createdAt": "2026-11-04T...Z"
}
```

错误码：

| 状态码 | 触发条件 | 行为 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 201 | 输入合法、重算成功、INSERT 成功 | 返回 `RunRecord` |
| 400 | 缺字段、非法枚举、重复防御、顶层或 `conditions` 多余字段、携带 `result` | Fastify 默认 400；不调用引擎、不写库 |
| 500 | 引擎异常或 SQLite 写入失败 | 返回通用 `{ statusCode:500, error:"Internal Server Error", message:"保存运行失败" }`；不产生半条记录 |

重复点击保存允许产生多条独立记录；按钮在保存中禁用，服务端不做幂等去重。

### `GET /api/runs/:id`

- 成功返回 **200**，响应同为 `RunRecord`。
- `:id` 为不透明字符串，不强制 UUID 格式；库中不存在即 **404**，返回 `{ statusCode:404, error:"Not Found", message:"运行记录不存在" }`。
- 数据库读取异常返回 **500**，message 为 `"读取运行记录失败"`，不把 SQL 细节返回给前端。
- 不支持列表接口，也不支持 PUT/DELETE。

## 2. SQLite 位置、测试隔离与关闭释放

### 文件位置与推导

新增 `apps/api/src/db.ts`，默认路径由 `import.meta.url` 推导，不依赖 `process.cwd()`：

```ts
const repoRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../", import.meta.url));
const defaultDbPath = resolve(repoRoot, ".data", "link-lab.db");
```

推导方式：

- src 启动：`file:///d:/ago/link/apps/api/src/db.ts`，向上三级为 `d:/ago/link/`。
- dist 启动：`file:///d:/ago/link/apps/api/dist/db.js`，向上三级同样为 `d:/ago/link/`。
- 因此两种启动都落到 `d:/ago/link/.data/link-lab.db`。
- `dist/` 与 `src/` 目录层级严格镜像，`db.ts`、`explanation.ts`、`app.ts`、`server.ts` 都放在同一级，避免新增子目录后相对层级失配。

路径优先级：

1. `buildServer` 显式传入的 `dbPath`（测试用）。
2. 环境变量 `DATABASE_PATH`：绝对路径直接用；相对路径以 `repoRoot` 为基准解析，不用 `apps/api` 的 cwd。
3. 默认 `repoRoot/.data/link-lab.db`。

`.gitignore` 已忽略 `*.db` 和 `.data/`，无需新增忽略规则。

### 表结构

启动时执行一次 `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`，保存时只做一条 `INSERT`：

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runs (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  scenario_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  scenario_version INTEGER NOT NULL,
  input_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  result_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  explanation TEXT NOT NULL,
  explanation_version INTEGER NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);
```

`input_json`、`result_json` 用 `JSON.stringify` 原样保存；`scenario_id` 为后续 Day 13 列表预留，不额外拆条件列。

### 测试隔离

- 现有 `apps/api/tests/app.test.ts` 改为 `buildServer({ logger:false }, { dbPath: ":memory:" })`，每个测试文件独立内存库。
- 新持久化测试使用 `mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "link-lab-"))` 建临时目录，显式传入 `.../link-lab.db`；`afterAll` 先 `app.close()` 再 `rmSync` 临时目录。Windows 上先释放连接再删文件。
- 所有测试都不触碰仓库根 `.data/`，不读取真实数据库。
- `buildServer` 第二参数为 `{ dbPath?: string }`；不传时走默认路径，现有调用方式保持兼容。

### 关闭释放

- `buildServer` 内 `app.addHook("onClose", async () => db.close())`，带已关闭标记，重复 close 不抛错。
- `src/server.ts` 监听 `SIGINT`、`SIGTERM`，触发 `app.close()` 后退出。
- `listen` 失败时先 `app.close()` 再 `process.exit(1)`。
- 测试中 `afterAll` 必调 `app.close()`。

## 3. 保存时由服务器重算并一次写入

- `POST /api/runs` 处理器只接受 `RunInput`；即使前端伪造 `result`/`explanation`，400 阶段就被拒绝。
- 调用现有 `evaluateRun(scenario, request.body as RunInput)`，与 `/api/evaluations` 使用同一场景对象和同一引擎，前端提交的结论不被信任。
- `scenarioVersion` 取服务器内 `scenario.version`，不取请求字段。
- 新增 `apps/api/src/explanation.ts`，纯函数 `buildRunExplanation(result)`，只读已重算的 `result`，不调用外部 AI、不引入随机数或时间：
  - 两条均阻断：写出 A/B 各自的 `firstBlockedStepId` 与原样 `blockedReason`。
  - A 到达、B 阻断：写明 B 阻断原因，并说明 A 仍到达模拟数据。
  - A 阻断、B 到达：对称处理。
  - 两条均到达：说明当前配置未阻断任何已建模路径。
  - 末尾用分号补充告警事实：`alertCount > 0` 时写“D3 记录了 N 条敏感请求告警，但未自动阻断”，否则写“本次未产生告警”。
  - 整句以“。”结束，不出现“系统已安全”“实战”“替代靶场”等表述。
- `explanationVersion` 固定常量 `1`，来源固定为规则模板，不做模型解释，也不做版本迁移。
- 生成 `id = randomUUID()`、`createdAt = new Date().toISOString()` 后，用一条预编译 `INSERT` 同时写入 input/result/scenarioVersion/explanation/explanationVersion/createdAt。INSERT 失败即无记录。

## 4. `GET` 只读库，不重新推演、不重新生成解释

处理器只执行一条预编译语句：

```sql
SELECT id, input_json, result_json, scenario_version, explanation, explanation_version, created_at
FROM runs WHERE id = ?
```

- 不调用 `evaluateRun`。
- 不调用 `buildRunExplanation`。
- 不执行 UPDATE、DELETE、PRAGMA 或建表；建表只发生在服务启动阶段。
- 直接把 `input_json`/`result_json` 解析后返回，`scenarioVersion`、`explanation` 原样来自库。
- 测试会用第二条连接直接改写某条记录的 `result_json` 与 `explanation` 为哨兵值，再 GET，断言返回值就是哨兵值且行数不变，证明没有重算或重写解释。

## 5. 工作台保存按钮与旧请求防串挂

只改 `apps/app/src` 下的 `App.tsx` 与 `api.ts`，不新增组件。

### 只保存当前成功运行

- `api.ts` 新增 `saveRun(input: RunInput): Promise<RunRecord>`，POST `/api/runs`，错误处理与现有 `postEvaluation` 同风格。
- 保存按钮只出现在 `runState.status === "done"` 的结果区域内；`idle/running/error` 时不渲染或禁用。
- 点击时构造请求体只使用 `runState.input`（那次成功 POST 的快照），不使用 `conditionsRef`、`defensesRef` 或滑块 `preview`，因此拖动未松手的新预览不会被保存。
- 请求体不包含 `result`，服务端必然重算。
- 基线补跑、滑块提交、防御切换都只走 `run()`/`runBaseline()`，不调用 `saveRun`。

### 旧保存响应不挂到新配置

新增保存状态与闸门：

```ts
type SaveState =
  | { status: "idle" }
  | { status: "saving" }
  | { status: "saved"; record: RunRecord; epoch: number; configKey: string }
  | { status: "error"; message: string; epoch: number; configKey: string };
```

- 点击保存时同步捕获：
  - `saveRunVersion = runVersionRef.current`
  - `saveEpoch = runEpoch`
  - `configKey = serializeConfig(input.conditions, input.defenses)`
  - `saveToken = ++saveTokenRef.current`
- 响应返回后必须同时满足才允许写入界面：
  - `saveToken === saveTokenRef.current`
  - `saveRunVersion === runVersionRef.current`
  - 组件仍挂载
  - 当前 `runEpoch` 仍等于捕获值（渲染时用于展示成功态）
  - 当前 `runState.status === "done"` 且其 input 的 `configKey` 仍等于捕获值
- 任一条件不满足就静默丢弃，不显示“保存成功”，也不覆盖新配置的错误或空闲状态。
- `run()` 开始、`handleSelectPreset()`、`loadScenario()` 时：`saveTokenRef` 递增、`setSaveState({status:"idle"})`。因此保存过程中触发新运行或切预设，旧响应回来只会被丢弃；播放、上一步、下一步、跳转不改保存状态，因为它们仍属于同一次运行。
- 保存中按钮禁用；保存成功显示 `已保存运行 #id` 与服务端返回的一句解释；失败显示错误，不显示假成功。
- “学习记录”页当前文案“当前版本不保存任何运行”会失真，改为“可在工作台保存当前运行；历史列表与回放后续开放”，仍不实现列表与回放。

三处修正已确认，均纳入计划；未写文件、未运行修改命令。

**1. SQLite 路径与忽略规则**

- 默认库改为 `apps/api/data/link-lab.db`，由 `import.meta.url` 推导：
  `new URL("../data/link-lab.db", import.meta.url)`
- `apps/api/src/db.ts` 与 `apps/api/dist/db.js` 都位于 `apps/api/{src|dist}/`，因此 `../data` 在两种启动方式下都解析到 `d:/ago/link/apps/api/data/link-lab.db`。
- 目录不存在时用 `mkdirSync(dirname(dbPath), { recursive: true })` 自动创建；`:memory:` 与已有测试临时目录不执行创建。
- 优先级保持：显式 `dbPath` > `DATABASE_PATH` > 默认路径。

实际看到的 `.gitignore` 原文：

```text
node_modules/
dist/
*.tsbuildinfo

# 本地数据库
*.db
*.db-journal
*.sqlite
*.sqlite3
.data/

# 环境变量（保留模板）
.env
.env.*
!.env.example
```

最终在“# 本地数据库”段落后新增的行：

```text
# SQLite 本地数据目录（含 -wal / -shm 等临时文件）
apps/api/data/
```

目录级忽略会覆盖 `apps/api/data/link-lab.db`、`link-lab.db-wal`、`link-lab.db-shm` 等所有子文件，不再依赖其他现有规则。

**2. 契约字段对齐**

`packages/contracts/src/records.ts` 中 `RunSnapshot` 必备字段统一为：

```ts
id: string;
savedAt: string;
scenarioId: string;
scenarioVersion: number;
input: RunInput;
result: RunResult;
explanation: string;
explanationSource: "template";
explanationVersion: number;
```

`RunRecord extends RunSnapshot`，不增删、不改名任何上述字段；`SaveRunResponse = RunRecord`。全部废弃 `createdAt`，数据库列改为 `saved_at`，并新增 `explanation_source`，保存时写入字面量 `"template"`。

前端成功提示显示“一句话解释（规则模板）：……”，其中“规则模板”由 `record.explanationSource` 映射得到，不无条件写死；提示同时显示 `record.id`、`record.savedAt`。

**3. GET 不重算的哨兵测试**

该测试强制使用临时文件库，不使用 `:memory:`：

```text
mkdtemp 临时目录
→ 服务器 A 使用 .../link-lab.db 保存一条记录
→ A.close()
→ 用 node:sqlite 新连接直接 UPDATE 该行 result_json 与 explanation 为哨兵值
→ 服务器 B 打开同一文件 GET 该 id
→ 断言返回哨兵值，且 runs 行数不变
→ B.close() 后清理临时目录
```

其余保存、重启读取、400 不落库测试同样走临时文件库；现有 `app.test.ts` 的 `:memory:` 只用于保持原接口测试隔离。

Design db.ts:

```ts
import { mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { dirname, isAbsolute, resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { DatabaseSync, type StatementSync } from "node:sqlite";
import type { RunInput, RunRecord, RunResult } from "@link-lab/contracts";

const repoRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../", import.meta.url));

export function resolveDatabasePath(
  moduleUrl: string | URL = import.meta.url,
  databasePath: string | undefined = process.env.DATABASE_PATH
): string {
  if (databasePath && databasePath.trim() !== "") {
    return isAbsolute(databasePath) ? databasePath : resolve(repoRoot, databasePath);
  }
  return fileURLToPath(new URL("../data/link-lab.db", moduleUrl));
}
```
Wait default via moduleUrl: `new URL("../data/link-lab.db", moduleUrl)`. If moduleUrl is default import.meta.url yes. For tests passing file URL works. const fileUrl = typeof moduleUrl === "string" ? new URL(moduleUrl) : moduleUrl;
const repoRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../", fileUrl));
```
But careful: fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../", fileUrl)) returns trailing separator? `fileURLToPath` returns `d:\ago\link\`. For resolve, trailing sep okay. Default path `fileURLToPath(new URL("../data/link-lab.db", fileUrl))`.

POST:
```ts
app.post("/api/runs", { schema: { body: runInputSchema } }, async (request, reply) => {
  const input = request.body as RunInput;
  try {
    const result = evaluateRun(scenario, input);
    const savedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    const record: RunRecord = {
      id: randomUUID(),
      savedAt,
      scenarioId: scenario.id,
      scenarioVersion: scenario.version,
      input,
      result,
      explanation: buildRunExplanation(result),
      explanationSource: EXPLANATION_SOURCE,
      explanationVersion: EXPLANATION_VERSION
    };
    runStore.insert(record);
    return reply.code(201).send(record);
  } catch (error) {
    request.log.error(error);
    return reply.code(500).send({ statusCode:500, error:"Internal Server Error", message:"保存运行失败" });
  }
});
```

Implementation template code:

```ts
const ALERT_ZERO = "本次未产生告警。";
function alertClause(alertCount: number): string {
  return alertCount > 0
    ? `本次 D3 记录了 ${alertCount} 条敏感请求告警，但未自动阻断。`
    : "本次未产生告警。";
}

function blockedSentence(pathResult: PathResult): string {
  if (!pathResult.firstBlockedStepId || !pathResult.blockedReason) {
    throw new Error(`路径 ${pathResult.pathId} 的阻断信息不完整`);
  }
  return `路径 ${pathResult.pathId} 在 ${pathResult.firstBlockedStepId} 被阻断（${pathResult.blockedReason}）`;
}

export function buildRunExplanation(result: RunResult): string {
  const pathA = result.pathResults.find(p=>p.pathId==="A");
  const pathB = ...
  if (!pathA || !pathB) throw ...
  let body: string;
  if (pathA.outcome==="blocked" && pathB.outcome==="blocked") {
    body = `两条已建模路径均未到达模拟数据：${blockedSentence(pathA)}；${blockedSentence(pathB)}`;
  } else if (pathA.outcome==="blocked") {
    body = `${blockedSentence(pathA)}，但路径 B 仍到达模拟数据`;
  } else if (pathB.outcome==="blocked") {
    body = `${blockedSentence(pathB)}，但路径 A 仍到达模拟数据`;
  } else {
    body = "两条已建模路径均到达模拟数据，当前配置未阻断任何已建模路径";
  }
  return `${body}；${alertClause(result.alertCount)}`;
}
```

import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { RunInput, RunRecord } from "@link-lab/contracts";
import { buildServer } from "../src/app";
import { resolveDatabasePath } from "../src/db";

const baseInput: RunInput = { scenarioId:"campus-registration", conditions:{O:"open",T:"granted"}, defenses:["D1","D3"] };

function countRows(dbPath: string): number {
 const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath);
 try { const row = db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM runs").get() as { count: number|bigint }; return Number(row.count); } finally { db.close(); }
}
function postRun(app, input) { return app.inject({method:"POST", url:"/api/runs", payload: input as RunInput}); }
```

Function signature:
```ts
export function resolveDatabasePath(
  moduleUrl: string | URL = import.meta.url,
  databasePath: string | undefined = process.env.DATABASE_PATH
): string {
  const fileUrl = typeof moduleUrl === "string" ? new URL(moduleUrl) : moduleUrl;
  const repoRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../", fileUrl));
  const configured = databasePath?.trim();
  if (configured) return isAbsolute(configured) ? configured : resolve(repoRoot, configured);
  return fileURLToPath(new URL("../data/link-lab.db", fileUrl));
}
```

```ts
import { buildServer } from "./app.ts";

const port = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 3001);
const app = buildServer();

let closing = false;
async function shutdown(signal: string): Promise<void> {
  if (closing) return;
  closing = true;
  app.log.info(`收到 ${signal}，关闭服务`);
  try { await app.close(); } finally { process.exit(0); }
}
process.once("SIGINT", () => void shutdown("SIGINT"));
process.once("SIGTERM", () => void shutdown("SIGTERM"));

try { await app.listen({host:"127.0.0.1",port}); } catch(error) { app.log.error(error); await app.close(); process.exit(1);}
```

## 要求贴出的代码原文

**[api.ts](file:///d:/ago/link/apps/web/src/api.ts) 中 `saveRun` 完整实现**

```ts
export async function saveRun(input: RunInput): Promise<RunRecord> {
  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetch("/api/runs", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(input)
    });
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(
      `无法连接后端：${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
    );
  }
  if (!response.ok) await throwHttpError(response);
  return (await response.json()) as RunRecord;
}
```

**[App.tsx](file:///d:/ago/link/apps/web/src/App.tsx) 中保存闸门判断原文**

```tsx
  /**
   * 显式保存当前成功运行。只发送该次 runState.input 快照；
   * 响应返回时若 token/runVersion 已变（新运行、切预设、场景重载），静默丢弃。
   */
  async function handleSaveRun() {
    if (runState.status !== "done") return;
    if (saveInFlightRef.current) return;
    const input = runState.input;
    const version = runVersionRef.current;
    const token = ++saveTokenRef.current;
    saveInFlightRef.current = true;
    setSaveState({ status: "saving" });
    try {
      const record = await saveRun(input);
      if (token !== saveTokenRef.current || version !== runVersionRef.current) return;
      if (!mountedRef.current) return;
      setSaveState({ status: "saved", record, runVersion: version });
    } catch (error) {
      if (token !== saveTokenRef.current || version !== runVersionRef.current) return;
      if (!mountedRef.current) return;
      setSaveState({
        status: "error",
        message: errorMessage(error),
        runVersion: version
      });
    } finally {
      saveInFlightRef.current = false;
    }
  }
```

失效点（`run()`、`handleSelectPreset()`、`loadScenario()` 内调用）：

```tsx
  /** 使在途保存响应与保存展示失效：新运行、切预设、场景重载时调用。 */
  function invalidateSave() {
    saveTokenRef.current++;
    setSaveState({ status: "idle" });
  }
```

展示闸门（成功/错误均要求 runVersion 仍为当前轮）：

```tsx
{saveState.status === "saved" &&
saveState.runVersion === runVersionRef.current ? ( ... ) : null}
{saveState.status === "error" &&
saveState.runVersion === runVersionRef.current ? ( ... ) : null}
```

成功提示的来源标签取自响应字段：

```tsx
const EXPLANATION_SOURCE_LABELS: Record<RunRecord["explanationSource"], string> = {
  template: "规则模板"
};

function explanationSourceLabel(source: RunRecord["explanationSource"]): string {
  return EXPLANATION_SOURCE_LABELS[source] ?? source;
}
```

  - To be safe, request body includes `runId`? Route includes id; plus `prediction`, `answers`, `eventIndex`, and `expectedConfig` (O/T/defenses) derived from UI snapshot. Server compares to stored run input and returns 409 `condition-mismatch` if not. Or 400? Rather 409 conflict. Need decide.

- `GET /api/runs/:id/learning-record` returns latest or 404. Response includes `run` snapshot? To satisfy "刷新后从学习记录页读回配置、答案、反馈、事件与原解释；读取时仍使用关联运行的原始快照." #### App.tsx 前端设计

**新增状态**（与工作台完全独立）：
```
recordsList: { status: "idle" | "loading" | "error" | "done"; summaries?: RunSummary[] }
selectedRecord: RunRecord | null
selectedEventIndex: number
selectedPlaying: boolean
selectedRunEpoch: number
selectedFetchTokenRef: useRef(0)   // 取消上一条详情请求
selectedRunTimerRef: useRef<number | null>  // 取消上一条播放定时器
```

**生命周期钩子**：
- `page === "records"` 切换时：调用 `listRuns()`，**取消工作台播放**（`setPlaying(false)`）
- 点击列表项：`selectedFetchTokenRef++` 取消上一条请求 → `GET /api/runs/:id` → 存入 `selectedRecord` → `selectedEventIndex=0, selectedPlaying=false, selectedRunEpoch++`
- `page !== "records"`（离开记录页）：`selectedRecord=null, selectedPlaying=false, selectedRunEpoch++`，**不写回工作台 runState/conditions/defenses**

**回放视图**：
- `selectedRecord !== null` 且 `selectedRecord.scenarioVersion === scenario.version` → 复用 `ScenarioGraph` + `CurrentEventPanel` + `PlaybackBar`
- `selectedRecord.scenarioVersion !== scenario.version` → 显示 warning "当前场景版本不一致，无法用现有画布回放"，**仅用 `CurrentEventPanel` 文字回放**，隐藏 `ScenarioGraph`

**独立播放定时器**（与工作台的 `playing`/`eventIndex` 完全独立的 `useEffect`）：
```
useEffect([selectedPlaying, selectedEventIndex, selectedRecord, selectedRunEpoch])
```
cleanup 时 `window.clearInterval(id)`，每次 `selectedRunEpoch` 变化时旧定时器自动失效。

**约束**：
- 回放过程中**零 POST**（`/api/runs`、`/api/evaluations` 均不出现）
- 离开记录页不污染工作台的 `runState`、`conditions`、`defenses`、`baselineState`

#### 后端反馈生成逻辑（纯函数，不写库）

```
buildDeterministicFeedback(runResult, predictions):
  Q1: 预设A + D1 only → pathB outcome === "reached" → correct
      条件依据：D1 仅阻断 A3，路径 B 无阻断
  Q2: 预设A + D3 only → pathA/outcome="reached" && pathB/outcome="reached" && alertCount=2 → correct  
      条件依据：D3 只产告警不阻断
  Q3: 预设B + D1 only → pathB blockedReason 含 "O" 或 diagnostic → correct
      条件依据：预设B O=closed，B2 因 O 关闭被拒，后续未执行
```

**硬约束**：后端**永远不信任**前端提交的 `answers` 或 `feedbacks`。前端请求体只含 `predictions`（用户选了什么），后端用引擎结果自己算正确答案和反馈。

### 段 C：显式版本核对

#### 新增/修改文件清单

| # | 文件 | 操作 | 行数变化 | 说明 |
|---|------|------|---------|------|
| 1 | `packages/contracts/src/verify.ts` | **新建** | +30~40 | 导出 `VerifyStatus = "match" | "result-mismatch" | "version-mismatch"`、`VerifyResponse`（含 status、可选 expected/actual、可选 message）。 |
| 2 | `packages/contracts/src/index.ts` | **追加 1 行** | +1, -0 | `export type { ... } from "./verify.js";` |
| 3 | `apps/api/src/app.ts` | **追加路由** | +40~50 | 新增 `POST /api/runs/:id/verify`。伪代码见下方。 |
| 4 | `apps/web/src/api.ts` | **追加** | +10 | `verifyRun(id): Promise<VerifyResponse>`。 |
| 5 | `apps/web/src/App.tsx` | **追加** | +30~50 | 回放页新增"核对当前版本"按钮，显示核对结果。 |
| 6 | `apps/api/tests/verify.test.ts` | **新建** | +120~180 | 三种返回状态、before === after 断言、直接读测试库核对三字段原文不变。 |

#### 核对路由伪代码

```typescript
app.post("/api/runs/:id/verify", async (request, reply) => {
  const stored = runStore.findById(id);
  if (!stored) return reply.code(404).send({ statusCode: 404, ... });
  
  // 版本不一致 → 直接返回，不调用引擎
  if (stored.scenarioId !== scenario.id || stored.scenarioVersion !== scenario.version) {
    return { status: "version-mismatch" };
  }
  
  // 版本一致 → 用存储的 input 调当前引擎
  const recomputed = evaluateRun(scenario, stored.input);
  
  // 深度比较完整 RunResult
  const match = deepEqual(recomputed, stored.result);
  
  return match 
    ? { status: "match" }
    : { status: "result-mismatch", expected: stored.result, actual: recomputed };
});
```

#### 零副作用测试用例（必须通过）

```
1. 创建一条记录 → GET detail before → POST verify → GET detail after
   → assert before.input_json === after.input_json
   → assert before.result_json === after.result_json  
   → assert before.explanation === after.explanation
   → 直接打开测试库 SELECT → 三字段原文不变

2. 覆盖 match 场景：用当前场景版本创建的记录 → POST verify → status === "match"

3. 覆盖 version-mismatch：mock 一个 scenarioVersion=999 的记录 → POST verify 
   → status === "version-mismatch" → 断言 evaluateRun 未被调用（mock 验证）
```

## 修订后的接口契约

### 段 A：列表与回放

**`GET /api/runs`** — 列表（仅摘要）

不调用 evaluateRun。SQL：`SELECT id, saved_at, scenario_id, scenario_version, input_json, result_json FROM runs ORDER BY saved_at DESC, rowid DESC`

返回 `RunSummary[]`，每条含：
- `id: string`
- `savedAt: string`（ISO）
- `scenarioId: string`
- `scenarioVersion: number`
- `conditions: { O, T }` — 从 input_json 解析
- `defenses: DefenseId[]` — 从 input_json 解析
- `pathResults: PathResult[]` — 从 result_json 解析，含 `pathId`、`outcome`、可选 `firstBlockedStepId`/`blockedReason`
- `alertCount: number` — 从 result_json 解析

**`GET /api/runs/:id`** — 沿用 Day 12 语义（九字段完整快照），**不新增字段**。

**版本不一致时的文字回放规则**：`CurrentEventPanel` 的 nodeLabel 函数切换为"直接返回 fromNodeId / toNodeId 原值"，不查当前场景的中文标签。同版本才用场景 nodeLabel。

---

### 段 B：三题与学习记录

**题目元数据（固定，写在 `apps/api/src/learning.ts`）**：

| QID | 题干 | 固定配置 | correctOption（正确答案序号） |
|-----|------|---------|---------------------------|
| Q1 | 预设 A 仅启用 D1 时，报名明细是否仍可到达？ | `{ scenarioId: "campus-registration", presetId: "A", conditions: { O: "open", T: "granted" }, defenses: ["D1"] }` | option 0 |
| Q2 | 预设 A 仅启用 D3 时，数据是否被阻断？ | `{ ...presetA, defenses: ["D3"] }` | option 2 |
| Q3 | 预设 B 只开 D1，是否还必须启用 D2 才能阻断两条已建模路径？ | `{ ...presetB（O=closed）, defenses: ["D1"] }` | option 0 |

每题 option 结构：`{ id: string, text: string }`（Q1 四个选项、Q2 三个选项、Q3 两个选项）。

**`buildDeterministicFeedback(predictions)` — 纯函数，在 `apps/api/src/learning.ts`**

对每题独立调用 evaluateRun（用该题固定配置，不依赖任何运行记录的 input）：

```
Q1: result = evaluateRun(scenario, Q1固定配置)
    correctOption = result.pathResults.find(p => p.pathId === "B")?.outcome === "reached" → option 0
    isCorrect = predictions["Q1"] === correctOption
    conditionBasis = "D1 仅阻断路径 A（A3），路径 B 无阻断，仍到达"

Q2: result = evaluateRun(scenario, Q2固定配置)
    bothReached = result.pathResults.every(p => p.outcome === "reached")
    alertCount   = result.alertCount === 2
    correctOption = bothReached && alertCount → option 2
    isCorrect = predictions["Q2"] === correctOption  
    conditionBasis = "D3 只产告警不阻断，A/B 均到达，alertCount=2"

Q3: result = evaluateRun(scenario, Q3固定配置)
    b2Event = result.events.find(e => e.pathId === "B" && e.stepId === "B2")
    oCheck  = b2Event?.conditionChecks.find(c => c.id === "O")
    bBlockedByO = oCheck && !oCheck.satisfied
    correctOption = bBlockedByO → option 0
    isCorrect = predictions["Q3"] === correctOption
    conditionBasis 文案 = oCheck.reason（从引擎 conditionChecks.reason 取，不另写死）
```

**`POST /api/learning`** — 创建/覆盖学习记录

请求体 `SaveLearningRequest`：
```
{
  runId: string,                    // 必须，关联已保存运行
  predictions: Record<"Q1"|"Q2"|"Q3", string>  // 用户选项（option id），不含 answers/feedbacks
}
```

处理逻辑：
1. `runStore.findById(runId)` → 不存在返回 404
2. 对每题用固定配置调用 evaluateRun → 算 correctOption、isCorrect、conditionBasis、engineOutcome
3. 同一 runId 已有记录 → **覆盖更新**（upsert 语义）
4. 写入 `learning_records` 表，返回完整 `LearningRecord`

**`GET /api/runs/:id/learning`** — 获取学习记录（新增路由）

- 有记录返回 200 + `LearningRecord`
- 无记录返回 404（不是 null）。前端收到 404 显示"这条运行还没有学习记录"

**学习记录表字段**：`id`、`run_id`（TEXT 外键，应用层校验存在）、`questions_json`（题目元数据快照）、`predictions_json`、`answers_json`（后端算的 correctOption）、`feedbacks_json`（含 isCorrect/conditionBasis/engineOutcome）、`saved_at`、`resumed_event_index`（可空，恢复时的播放下标）。

**合同**：同一 runId 多次 POST → **覆盖**。返回值中的 answers 和 feedbacks 完全由后端算，前端提交的 predictions 仅作为 isCorrect 的比较基准。

---

### 段 C：显式版本核对

**`POST /api/runs/:id/verify`**

返回状态：`"match"` | `"result-mismatch"` | `"version-mismatch"`

处理逻辑（零副作用，不写库、不生成解释、不改任何记录）：
```
1. runStore.findById(id) → 不存在返回 404
2. stored.scenarioId !== scenario.id || stored.scenarioVersion !== scenario.version
   → 直接返回 { status: "version-mismatch" }，不调用 evaluateRun
3. recomputed = evaluateRun(scenario, stored.input)
4. 深度比较：node:util.isDeepStrictEqual(recomputed, stored.result)
5. 返回 match 或 result-mismatch
```

**零副作用测试断言**：
- `before = GET detail` → `POST verify` → `after = GET detail` → `before === after`
- 直接打开测试库 `SELECT input_json, result_json, explanation FROM runs WHERE id = ?` → 三字段原文不变

### baselineRunId 建议

**维持单 runId，Day 13 文档记录这一简化**。

理由：
1. 干预运行的 RunRecord 已包含完整 input（conditions + defenses）和 result，回放配置、事件、解释都从这里取，学习记录核心需求已覆盖
2. 基线对比是工作台 Day 10 的增量分析功能，学习记录的主路径是"回放这条干预运行 + 学习三题"，基线对比不属于学习记录的必选项
3. 若后续学习记录页确实需要对比基线截图，可用 runId 反查同 conditions 且 defenses=[] 的 RunRecord，但这是可选增强，Day 13 不引入
4. 单 runId 避免新增关联复杂度，符合 Day 13 的增量范围

**未修改**：engine/src、scenario JSON、CSV、Day 12 POST/GET 语义、runs 表九字段。

### 二、关键代码原文

**db.ts — LIST_RUNS_SQL（纯 SQL，不调引擎）**：
```sql
SELECT id, saved_at, scenario_id, scenario_version, input_json, result_json
FROM runs ORDER BY saved_at DESC, rowid DESC
```

**app.ts — GET /api/runs 处理器**（只调 `runStore.listRuns()`，无 `evaluateRun`/`buildRunExplanation`）：
```typescript
app.get("/api/runs", async (request, reply) => {
  try {
    const summaries = runStore.listRuns();
    return { runs: summaries };
  } catch (error) {
    request.log.error(error);
    return reply.code(500).send({ statusCode: 500, error: "Internal Server Error", message: "读取运行列表失败" });
  }
});
```

**App.tsx — 独立播放定时器（selected playing vs workbench playing 完全隔离）**：
```typescript
useEffect(() => {
  if (!selectedPlaying || !selectedRecord) return;
  const events = selectedRecord.result.events;
  if (selectedEventIndex >= events.length - 1) { setSelectedPlaying(false); return; }
  const id = window.setInterval(() => { /* ...推进 index... */ }, 800);
  return () => window.clearInterval(id);
}, [selectedPlaying, selectedEventIndex, selectedRecord, selectedRunEpoch]);
```

**App.tsx — 版本不一致隔离**：版本不匹配时 `ScenarioGraph` 隐藏，`CurrentEventPanel` 的 `nodeLabel` 直接返回 `fromNodeId/toNodeId` 原值（不查当前场景中文标签）。

#### 改动 1：mock 列表追加两行 + 两个 import + 两个 alias

**位置**：[App.test.tsx#L6-L21](file:///D:/ago/link/apps/web/tests/App.test.tsx#L6-L21)

| 项 | 原文 | 改后 | 理由 |
|----|------|------|------|
| mock 列表 | `vi.mock(..., () => ({ fetchScenario, postEvaluation, saveRun }))` | `{ fetchScenario, postEvaluation, saveRun, listRuns, getRun }` 追加两行 | App.tsx 在 Day 13 引入了新依赖 `listRuns`/`getRun`，mock 必须完整导出，否则 vitest 报 `No "listRuns" export is defined on the "../src/api" mock` |
| import | `import { fetchScenario, postEvaluation, saveRun } from "../src/api";` | 追加 `getRun, listRuns` | 同上，ES module 导入必须包含实际使用的符号 |
| alias | `const fetchScenarioMock = ... const postEvaluationMock = ... const saveRunMock = ...` | 追加 `const getRunMock = ... const listRunsMock = ...` | 测试代码中后续需要直接访问 mock 实例（如 mockResolvedValue、toHaveBeenCalledTimes） |
| beforeEach | 无 listRuns 初始化 | 追加 `listRunsMock.mockResolvedValue([]);` | 默认空库，让旧测试在切换到 records 页时走空状态（不阻塞原有断言） |

**改动类型**：机械补全 mock 导出，不涉及测试逻辑变动。

| 测试名称 | `"顶部阶段标题更新为 Day 12，学习记录页如实说明保存支持"` | `"顶部阶段标题更新为 Day 12，学习记录页显示空状态而非旧占位符"` | 占位符已替换为真实空状态，测试名称随行为更新 |

Contract design details:

```ts
// learning.ts
import type { DefenseId, OValue, PathFinalOutcome, PresetId, RunInput, TValue, StepId, ConditionCheck } from "./index.js";

export type LearningQuestionId = "Q1" | "Q2" | "Q3";

export interface LearningOption {
  id: string;
  text: string;
}

/** 该题的固定配置：与工作台当前配置无关。 */
export interface LearningQuestionConfig {
  presetId: PresetId;
  conditions: { O: OValue; T: TValue };
  defenses: DefenseId[];
}

export interface LearningQuestion {
  id: LearningQuestionId;
  prompt: string;
  options: LearningOption[];
  config: LearningQuestionConfig;
}

/** 每题推演出的引擎事实（用于展示条件依据与核对）。 */
export interface LearningEngineEvidence {
  pathA: PathFinalOutcome;
  pathB: PathFinalOutcome;
  alertCount: number;
  blockedStepId?: StepId;
  conditionCheck?: ConditionCheck;
}

export interface LearningFeedback {
  questionId: LearningQuestionId;
  correctOption: string;
  predictedOption: string | null;
  isCorrect: boolean;
  conditionBasis: string;
  evidence: LearningEngineEvidence;
}

export interface LearningRecord {
  id: string;
  runId: string;
  savedAt: string;
  questions: LearningQuestion[];
  predictions: Partial<Record<LearningQuestionId, string>>;
  feedbacks: LearningFeedback[];
  resumedEventIndex: number | null;
}

export interface SaveLearningRequest {
  runId: string;
  predictions: Record<string, string>;
  resumedEventIndex?: number | null;
}

export type SaveLearningResponse = LearningRecord;

export interface ListLearningQuestionsResponse {
  questions: LearningQuestion[];
}
```

options:

- "3": "不能到达——路径 B 在 B2 处被 O 条件拒绝"

Q2 prompt: "预设 A（O 开、T 有）仅启用 D3 时，数据是否被阻断？"
options:
- "0": "被阻断——两条路径都被 D3 拦住"
- "1": "未被阻断——只有路径 A 可以到达"
- "2": "未被阻断——A/B 都能到达，并且出现告警" ← correct

Q3 prompt: "迁移题：预设 B（O 关、T 有）只启用 D1 时，是否还必须再启用 D2 才能阻断两条已建模路径？"
options:
- "0": "不必——B2 因 O 关闭被拒，后续步骤未执行，D1 已阻断路径 A" ← correct
- "1": "必须——否则路径 B 仍能到达报名明细"

Fixed configs: Q1 preset A conditions {O:open,T:granted} defenses [D1]; Q2 same + [D3]; Q3 preset B {O:closed,T:granted} defenses [D1].

Engine derivation:
- Q1: evaluateRun → pathResults find B → outcome === "reached" → derive "0" else "1"? Derivation should be robust but simple: 
  - if both blocked → option "1"; if B reached && alertCount > 0 → "2"? Hmm. Keep derivation targeted: 
  ```

  const pathAReached = ...

  ```
  Actually Q1 fixed config gives A blocked (D1), B reached, alertCount 0. Derivation: if B reached && A blocked && alertCount===0 → "0"; else pick by facts. For test equality only Q1's actual config matters. Simplest deterministic mapping: 
  - Q1: correctOption derived = B reached && alertCount === 0 ? "0" : alertCount > 0 ? "2" : "1".
  - Q2: derived = (both reached) ? (alertCount > 0 ? "2" : "1") : "0".
  - Q3: derived = (B2 O check unsatisfied) ? "0" : "1".

Test asserts derived === handwritten.

conditionBasis:
- Q1: use engine facts: `路径 A 在 A3 被 D1 阻断（${d1Check.reason}）；路径 B 无阻断，仍到达报名明细。` Should the basis text come from engine reasons? Requirement only mandates Q3's basis text from the check's reason; Q1/Q2 can be composed but should include actual values (Q2 alert count from actual). I'll compose from actual facts:
  - Q1: `路径 A 在 A3 被阻断：${a3check.reason}；路径 B 未命中阻断条件，最终到达报名明细。`
  - Q2: `D3 只记录告警、不阻断：A/B 均到达，本次推演共产生 ${alertCount} 条告警。`
  - Q3: `${oCheck.reason}；路径 B 在 B2 之后未执行，D1 已阻断路径 A。` — hmm the requirement says 条件依据文案也从这条 check 的 reason 取，不另写死. I'll set conditionBasis = `${oCheck.reason}（路径 B 在 B2 被拒绝，后续步骤未执行；D1 已阻断路径 A）`? That adds hardcoded text. The requirement: "条件依据的文案也从这条 check 的 reason 取，不另写死". I think basis should be exactly oCheck.reason, plus maybe dynamic facts. To be safe: basis = oCheck.reason. And evidence carries the rest (pathA blocked etc.) for UI display. But requirement from user earlier: "反馈同时展示条件依据". Display evidence separately. So Q3 conditionBasis = oCheck.reason exactly. For Q1/Q2 also take reasons from checks to avoid hardcoding? Q1: use A3 D1 check reason + pathB reached. Q2: has no failing check; use alert facts. Now DB: learning_records table:
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS learning_records (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  run_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  saved_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  questions_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  predictions_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  feedbacks_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  resumed_event_index INTEGER
)
```
Upsert:
```sql
INSERT INTO learning_records (id, run_id, saved_at, questions_json, predictions_json, feedbacks_json, resumed_event_index)
VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
ON CONFLICT(run_id) DO UPDATE SET
  id = excluded.id,
  saved_at = excluded.saved_at,
  questions_json = excluded.questions_json,
  predictions_json = excluded.predictions_json,
  feedbacks_json = excluded.feedbacks_json,
  resumed_event_index = excluded.resumed_event_index
```
Hmm updating id on conflict means the record id changes each submission. That's acceptable? "同一运行重复提交为覆盖，不保留历史答案". Updating id means GET by id would break if frontend cached old id. Frontend uses runId-based lookup so fine. But maybe keep original id to be stable? Using `DO UPDATE` with `id = excluded.id` is simpler but changes identity. Better: keep the original id stable, only update content: `saved_at = excluded.saved_at, ...` and NOT id. That preserves record identity. I'll do that (don't update id).

Wait but new id generated each POST would be discarded. That's fine; the stored id remains from first insert. Hmm — but then response returns which id? After upsert, we should read back the row to return the actual record (with stable id). Simpler: after upsert, `SELECT ... WHERE run_id = ?` and return it. That's an extra read but single statement requirement only applies to write ("不得先查再写"). Reading after write is fine.

```ts
app.post("/api/learning", { schema: { body: learningInputSchema } }, async (request, reply) => {
  const body = request.body as SaveLearningRequest;
  if (!runStore.findById(body.runId)) → 404 message "运行记录不存在"
  // validate predictions: exactly the three qids, each option exists
  if invalid → 400 "预测答案不合法"
  const feedbacks = buildLearningFeedback(scenario, body.predictions);
  const record = learningStore.upsert({...});
  return reply.code(200/201).send(record)  // 覆盖 → 200? Use 200 always.
});
```
Schema: body { runId: string (minLength 1), predictions: object, resumedEventIndex: integer|null optional }. Use JSON schema with additionalProperties false.

Note schema validation errors return 400 automatically with Fastify default. Good.

```ts
app.get("/api/runs/:id/learning", async (request, reply) => {
  const run = runStore.findById(id);
  if (!run) return 404 { message: "运行记录不存在" }
  const learning = learningStore.findByRunId(id);
  if (!learning) return 404 { message: "该运行暂无学习记录" }
  return learning;
});
```

Distinguishability: front-end maps: message contains "暂无学习记录" → empty state. But api.ts throwHttpError builds `HTTP 404：{"statusCode":404,...}` string. Better to have typed client: `getRunLearning` catches 404 by reading JSON, and returns `null` if message === "该运行暂无学习记录", otherwise throw. That's a clean way: client distinguishes by response body. Implement:

```ts
export async function getRunLearning(runId: string): Promise<LearningRecord | null> {
  const response = await fetch(`/api/runs/${runId}/learning`);
  if (response.status === 404) {
    const body = await response.json() as { message?: string };
    if (body.message === "该运行暂无学习记录") return null;
    throw new Error(`HTTP 404：${JSON.stringify(body)}`);
  }
  if (!response.ok) await throwHttpError(response);
  return ...
}
```

Good — frontend cannot confuse run-not-exist with no-learning.

Now db.ts: add learning table + store.

Design:
```ts
const LEARNING_TABLE_SQL = `
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS learning_records (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    run_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    saved_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    questions_json TEXT NOT NULL,
    predictions_json TEXT NOT NULL,
    feedbacks_json TEXT NOT NULL,
    resumed_event_index INTEGER
  )
`;

const UPSERT_LEARNING_SQL = `
  INSERT INTO learning_records (id, run_id, saved_at, questions_json, predictions_json, feedbacks_json, resumed_event_index)
  VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  ON CONFLICT(run_id) DO UPDATE SET
    saved_at = excluded.saved_at,
    questions_json = excluded.questions_json,
    predictions_json = excluded.predictions_json,
    feedbacks_json = excluded.feedbacks_json,
    resumed_event_index = excluded.resumed_event_index
`;

const SELECT_LEARNING_BY_RUN_SQL = `
  SELECT id, run_id, saved_at, questions_json, predictions_json, feedbacks_json, resumed_event_index
  FROM learning_records WHERE run_id = ?
`;
```

Careful: node:sqlite DatabaseSync supports ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE (standard SQLite). Yes.

Routes:
```ts
app.get("/api/learning/questions", async () => ({ questions: LEARNING_QUESTIONS }));

app.post("/api/learning", { schema: { body: learningInputSchema } }, async (request, reply) => {
  const body = request.body as SaveLearningRequest;
  try {
    if (!runStore.findById(body.runId)) {
      return reply.code(404).send({ statusCode: 404, error: "Not Found", message: "运行记录不存在" });
    }
    const invalid = validatePredictions(body.predictions);
    if (invalid) {
      return reply.code(400).send({ statusCode: 400, error: "Bad Request", message: invalid });
    }
    const feedbacks = buildLearningFeedback(scenario, body.predictions);
    const record: LearningRecord = {
      id: randomUUID(),
      runId: body.runId,
      savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      questions: LEARNING_QUESTIONS,
      predictions: body.predictions,
      feedbacks,
      resumedEventIndex: body.resumedEventIndex ?? null
    };
    return reply.code(200).send(learningStore.upsert(record));
  } catch (error) { ... 500 "保存学习记录失败" }
});

app.get("/api/runs/:id/learning", async (request, reply) => {
  const { id } = request.params as { id: string };
  try {
    if (!runStore.findById(id)) {
      return reply.code(404).send({ ... message: "运行记录不存在" });
    }
    const record = learningStore.findByRunId(id);
    if (!record) {
      return reply.code(404).send({ ... message: "该运行暂无学习记录" });
    }
    return record;
  } catch { 500 "读取学习记录失败" }
});
```

Add helper functions:

```ts
function questionConfigText(question: LearningQuestion): string {
  const defenseText = question.config.defenses.length > 0
    ? `防御 ${question.config.defenses.join("、")}`
    : "无防御";
  return `预设 ${question.config.presetId} · O ${oValueText[question.config.conditions.O]} · T ${tValueText[question.config.conditions.T]} · ${defenseText}`;
}

function optionTextOf(questions: LearningQuestion[], qid, optionId: string | null): string {
  if (optionId === null) return "未作答";
  const question = questions.find(q => q.id === qid);
  const option = question?.options.find(o => o.id === optionId);
  return option ? `${option.id}. ${option.text}` : optionId;
}

function outcomeText(outcome: PathFinalOutcome): string {
  return outcome === "reached" ? "到达" : "阻断";
}
```

Need to check label variables `oValueText`, `tValueText` exist in App (used in records list). Yes they were used in records list: `oValueText[sum.conditions.O]`. Good.

Feedback renderer:
```tsx
function renderFeedbackList(questions: LearningQuestion[], feedbacks: LearningFeedback[]) {
  return (
    <ul className="learning-feedbacks">
      {feedbacks.map(f => (
        <li key={f.questionId} className={f.isCorrect ? "feedback-correct" : "feedback-incorrect"}>
          <p><strong>{f.questionId}</strong> · {f.isCorrect ? "回答正确" : "回答错误"}</p>
          <p>你的预测：{optionTextOf(questions, f.questionId, f.predictedOption)}</p>
          <p>正确答案：{optionTextOf(questions, f.questionId, f.correctOption)}</p>
          <p>条件依据：{f.conditionBasis}</p>
          <p className="hint">
            引擎事实（按本题固定配置推演）：路径 A {outcomeText(f.evidence.pathA)} · 路径 B {outcomeText(f.evidence.pathB)} · 告警 {f.evidence.alertCount} 条
            {f.evidence.blockedStepId ? ` · 首个阻断步骤 ${f.evidence.blockedStepId}` : ""}
          </p>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```
Since defined inside component, it's fine (recreated each render, cheap).

Now JSX for quiz section:

```tsx
<div className="learning-quiz" aria-label="三题自测">
  <h3>三题自测</h3>
  <p className="hint">
    每题使用自己的固定配置独立推演，与上方工作台当前配置无关，也不评价眼前这次运行。
  </p>
  {questionsState.status === "loading" ? <p className="notice">正在加载题目…</p> : null}
  {questionsState.status === "error" ? (
    <div className="notice error" role="alert">
      <p>题目加载失败：{questionsState.message}</p>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => void fetchLearningQuestions()}>重试</button>
    </div>
  ) : null}
  {questionsState.status === "done" ? (
    <>
      {questionsState.questions.map(question => (
        <div className="quiz-question" key={question.id}>
          <p className="quiz-prompt"><strong>{question.id}</strong> · {question.prompt}</p>
          <p className="quiz-config">本题固定配置：{questionConfigText(question)}</p>
          <div className="quiz-options" role="radiogroup" aria-label={`${question.id} 选项`}>
            {question.options.map(option => (
              <button
                key={option.id}
                type="button"
                className={`quiz-option ${quizPredictions[question.id] === option.id ? "active" : ""}`}
                aria-pressed={quizPredictions[question.id] === option.id}
                disabled={quizState.status === "saving"}
                onClick={() => handlePredict(question.id, option.id)}
              >
                {option.id}. {option.text}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
      <div className="quiz-actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="run-button"
          disabled={
            quizState.status === "saving" ||
            saveState.status !== "saved" ||
            saveState.runVersion !== runVersionRef.current ||
            questionsState.questions.some(q => quizPredictions[q.id] === undefined)
          }
          onClick={() => void handleSubmitLearning()}
        >
          {quizState.status === "saving" ? "正在提交…" : "提交三题预测并保存学习记录"}
        </button>
        {saveState.status !== "saved" || saveState.runVersion !== runVersionRef.current ? (
          <p className="hint">先保存本次运行，才能把三题预测与这条运行关联。</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      {quizState.status === "error" && quizState.runVersion === runVersionRef.current ? (
        <p className="notice error" role="alert">提交失败：{quizState.message}</p>
      ) : null}
      {quizState.status === "done" && quizState.runVersion === runVersionRef.current ? (
        <>
          <p className="notice" role="status">学习记录已保存 #{quizState.record.id}（同一运行重复提交会覆盖，不保留历史答案）</p>
          {renderFeedbackList(quizState.record.questions, quizState.record.feedbacks)}
        </>
      ) : null}
    </>
  ) : null}
</div>
```

### 二、三题完整选项文本（供核对）

**Q1** 题干：预设 A（O 开放、T 有权限）仅启用 D1 时，报名明细是否仍可到达？
固定配置：`预设 A · O 开 · T 有 · D1`

| id | 文案 | 正确 |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | 能到达——路径 B 不受 D1 影响，仍可到达报名明细 | ✅ 手写=0 |
| 1 | 不能到达——路径 A 与路径 B 都被 D1 阻断 | |
| 2 | 能到达——但两条路径都会产生 D3 告警 | |
| 3 | 不能到达——路径 B 在 B2 处被 O 条件拒绝 | |

**Q2** 题干：预设 A（O 开放、T 有权限）仅启用 D3 时，数据是否被阻断？
固定配置：`预设 A · O 开 · T 有 · D3`

| id | 文案 | 正确 |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | 被阻断——两条路径都被 D3 拦住 | |
| 1 | 未被阻断——只有路径 A 可以到达报名明细 | |
| 2 | 未被阻断——A/B 都能到达，并且出现告警 | ✅ 手写=2 |

**Q3** 题干：迁移题：预设 B（O 关闭、T 有权限）只启用 D1 时，是否还必须再启用 D2 才能阻断两条已建模路径？
固定配置：`预设 B · O 关 · T 有 · D1`

| id | 文案 | 正确 |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | 不必——B2 因 O 关闭被拒，后续步骤未执行，D1 已阻断路径 A | ✅ 手写=0 |
| 1 | 必须——否则路径 B 仍能到达报名明细 | |

### 三、关键代码原文

**1. 手写答案 + 显式 isCorrect 比较**

```ts
export const HANDWRITTEN_CORRECT_OPTIONS: Record<LearningQuestionId, string> = {
  Q1: "0",
  Q2: "2",
  Q3: "0"
};
```

```ts
return {
  questionId: question.id,
  correctOption: derivation.optionId,
  predictedOption: predicted,
  isCorrect: predicted === derivation.optionId,
  conditionBasis: derivation.conditionBasis,
  evidence: derivation.evidence
};
```

**2. Q3 判据（取 B2 事件的 O 条件检查，不用 blockedReason 字符串）**

```ts
const b2 = result.events.find(
  (event) => event.pathId === "B" && event.stepId === "B2"
);
const oCheck = b2?.conditionChecks.find((check) => check.id === "O") ?? null;
const blockedByO = oCheck !== null && oCheck.satisfied === false;
const optionId = blockedByO ? "0" : "1";
const conditionBasis = oCheck ? oCheck.reason : "推演结果未提供 B2 的 O 条件检查说明。";
```

**3. Q2 文案用实际告警数（字面量 2 只出现在分支判断）**

```ts
const optionId = bothReached ? (result.alertCount > 0 ? "2" : "1") : "0";
const conditionBasis = bothReached
  ? `D3 只记录告警、不阻断：A/B 均到达，本次推演共产生 ${result.alertCount} 条告警。`
  : `本次推演中至少一条路径被阻断，共产生 ${result.alertCount} 条告警。`;
```

**4. 单条语句覆盖写入**

```sql
INSERT INTO learning_records (id, run_id, saved_at, questions_json, predictions_json, feedbacks_json, resumed_event_index)
VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
ON CONFLICT(run_id) DO UPDATE SET
  saved_at = excluded.saved_at,
  questions_json = excluded.questions_json,
  predictions_json = excluded.predictions_json,
  feedbacks_json = excluded.feedbacks_json,
  resumed_event_index = excluded.resumed_event_index
```

**5. 题目元数据不含答案 / 提交体拒绝越权字段**

```ts
app.get("/api/learning/questions", async () => ({ questions: LEARNING_QUESTIONS }));
```

```ts
const learningInputSchema = {
  type: "object",
  additionalProperties: false,
  required: ["runId", "predictions"],
  properties: { runId: {...}, predictions: {...}, resumedEventIndex: { anyOf: [{ type: "integer", minimum: 0 }, { type: "null" }] } }
};
```

**6. 404 语义区分**

```ts
if (!runStore.findById(id)) {
  return reply.code(404).send({ statusCode: 404, error: "Not Found", message: "运行记录不存在" });
}
const record = learningStore.findByRunId(id);
if (!record) {
  return reply.code(404).send({ statusCode: 404, error: "Not Found", message: "该运行暂无学习记录" });
}
```

```ts
// 前端：只有「该运行暂无学习记录」当正常空态
if (message === "该运行暂无学习记录") return null;
```

## 一、选项显示编号改为字母

**改动前**（[App.tsx](file:///D:/ago/link/apps/web/src/App.tsx#L1392-L1421) 答题区，以及 `optionTextOf`）：

```tsx
{question.options.map((option) => {
  ...
  <button
    key={option.id}
    className={`quiz-option ${selected ? "active" : ""}`}
    aria-pressed={selected}
    onClick={() => handlePredict(question.id, option.id)}
  >
    {option.id}. {option.text}
  </button>
```

```ts
const option = question?.options.find((item) => item.id === optionId);
return option ? `${option.id}. ${option.text}` : optionId;
```

**改动后**：

```tsx
{question.options.map((option, index) => {
  ...
  <button
    key={option.id}                       // key 仍用业务 id
    className={`quiz-option ${selected ? "active" : ""}`}
    aria-pressed={selected}
    onClick={() => handlePredict(question.id, option.id)}  // 提交仍是 option.id
  >
    {optionLetter(index)}. {option.text}
  </button>
```

```ts
/** 显示编号：由选项数组下标推导 A/B/C/D，与 option.id 无关。 */
function optionLetter(index: number): string {
  return String.fromCharCode(65 + index);
}

function optionTextOf(questions, questionId, optionId): string {
  if (optionId === null) return "未作答";
  const question = questions.find((item) => item.id === questionId);
  if (!question) return optionId;                 // 旧数据找不到题目 → 原样 id
  const index = question.options.findIndex((item) => item.id === optionId);
  if (index < 0) return optionId;                 // 旧数据找不到选项 → 原样 id，不空白
  return `${optionLetter(index)}. ${question.options[index].text}`;
}
```

硬性要求逐条核对：

- `option.id` 取值 `"0"/"1"/"2"/"3"` 未动，`handlePredict(question.id, option.id)` 与提交体 `predictions` 仍是原 id。
- 字母只由 `String.fromCharCode(65 + index)` 推导，没有任何 `{ "0": "A" }` 映射表。
- `HANDWRITTEN_CORRECT_OPTIONS`、schema、`apps/api/src/learning.ts`、数据库字段全部未改（`git status` 只有 3 个前端文件被修改）。
- 回放/反馈侧复用同一个 `optionTextOf`：反馈列表的「你的预测」「正确答案」以及记录页学习记录面板都通过 id 在 `questions` 数组里查位置再转字母；查不到即返回原始 id。
- 未删除或弱化任何断言。

## 二、CSS 差异

改动前该文件里**没有任何 `.quiz-option` 规则**（四个状态外观完全相同，全靠浏览器默认按钮样式），因此选中态与 hover/点击无区分。改动后新增（[styles.css](file:///D:/ago/link/apps/web/src/styles.css#L1179-L1232)）：

```css
.quiz-option { display:block; width:100%; text-align:left; padding:10px 12px;
  border:1px solid #2c3b49; border-radius:8px; background:#111b24; color:#c7d2dc;
  cursor:pointer; font-family:inherit; font-size:0.9rem; }

.quiz-option:hover  { background:#1d2a35; border-color:#47617a; }          /* 略亮 */

.quiz-option:active { background:#0b1219; border-color:#47617a;
  box-shadow: inset 0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.45); }                          /* 按下变暗 + 内凹 */

/* 选中态：更饱和背景 + 亮边框 + 勾选符号 + 加粗，不依赖颜色单一通道 */
.quiz-option.active { background:#1f4d63; border-color:#7fc8b5;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px #7fc8b5; color:#f2f7fa; font-weight:600; }

.quiz-option.active::before { content:"✓ "; color:#7fc8b5; font-weight:700; }

.quiz-option:disabled { opacity:.6; cursor:not-allowed; }
```

三态可肉眼区分：默认 `#111b24` 暗灰；hover `#1d2a35` 微亮；按下 `#0b1219` 明显变暗并内凹；选中 `#1f4d63` 深青高饱和 + `#7fc8b5` 亮边框 + `✓` 前缀 + 加粗。`.active` 规则排在 `:active` 之后，同时命中时选中样式胜出。`aria-pressed` 原样保留。未新增 `@keyframes`、未加 `transition`、未触碰任何既有动画规则或白名单类。

```ts
import type { RunRecord, RunResult, Scenario } from "@link-lab/contracts";
import { isDeepStrictEqual } from "node:util";
import { evaluateRun } from "@link-lab/engine";

export type VerifyOutcome = ... from contracts

export function verifyRunRecord(scenario, record): VerifyRunResponse {
  if (record.scenarioId !== scenario.id || record.scenarioVersion !== scenario.version) {
    return { status: "version-mismatch", recordScenarioId, currentScenarioId, recordScenarioVersion, currentScenarioVersion, differences: []? }
  }
  const recomputed = evaluateRun(scenario, record.input);
  if (!isDeepStrictEqual(recomputed, record.result)) {
    return { status: "result-mismatch", ..., differences: summarize... };
  }
  return { status: "match", ... };
}
```

**Route** in app.ts:

```ts
app.post("/api/runs/:id/verify", async (request, reply) => {
  const { id } = request.params as { id: string };
  try {
    const record = runStore.findById(id);
    if (!record) return 404 {message:"运行记录不存在"};
    return verifyRunRecord(scenario, record);
  } catch (error) { 500 "核对运行记录失败" }
});
```

Zero DB side effects — only findById.

VerifyRunResponse design:
```ts
export type VerifyStatus = "match" | "result-mismatch" | "version-mismatch";

export interface VerifyRunResponse {
  status: VerifyStatus;
  recordScenarioId: string;
  recordScenarioVersion: number;
  currentScenarioId: string;
  currentScenarioVersion: number;
  /** 仅在 result-mismatch 时给出差异摘要；不写入数据库。 */
  differences?: string[];
}
```

Index line: `export type { VerifyRunResponse, VerifyStatus } from "./verify.js";`

api verify.ts:
```ts
import { isDeepStrictEqual } from "node:util";
import type { RunRecord, RunResult, Scenario, VerifyRunResponse } from "@link-lab/contracts";
import { evaluateRun } from "@link-lab/engine";

function summarizeDifferences(stored: RunResult, recomputed: RunResult): string[] {
  const differences: string[] = [];
  if (stored.events.length !== recomputed.events.length) {
    differences.push(`事件数量不一致：快照 ${stored.events.length} 条 · 重算 ${recomputed.events.length} 条`);
  }
  const shared = Math.min(stored.events.length, recomputed.events.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < shared; i++) {
    if (!isDeepStrictEqual(stored.events[i], recomputed.events[i])) {
      differences.push(`事件 #${i} 不一致（${stored.events[i].pathId}-${stored.events[i].stepId}）`);
      break;  // 只列第一条差异事件，避免摘要刷屏
    }
  }
  if (!isDeepStrictEqual(stored.pathResults, recomputed.pathResults)) {
    differences.push("路径结论不一致");
  }
  if (stored.alertCount !== recomputed.alertCount) {
    differences.push(`告警数量不一致：快照 ${stored.alertCount} 条 · 重算 ${recomputed.alertCount} 条`);
  }
  if (differences.length === 0) {
    differences.push("结果存在字段级差异");
  }
  return differences;
}

export function verifyRunRecord(scenario: Scenario, record: RunRecord): VerifyRunResponse {
  const base = {
    recordScenarioId: record.scenarioId,
    recordScenarioVersion: record.scenarioVersion,
    currentScenarioId: scenario.id,
    currentScenarioVersion: scenario.version
  };
  if (record.scenarioId !== scenario.id || record.scenarioVersion !== scenario.version) {
    return { status: "version-mismatch", ...base };
  }
  const recomputed = evaluateRun(scenario, record.input);
  if (!isDeepStrictEqual(recomputed, record.result)) {
    return { status: "result-mismatch", ...base, differences: summarizeDifferences(record.result, recomputed) };
  }
  return { status: "match", ...base };
}
```

App.tsx:
- import type VerifyRunResponse; import verifyRun.
- type VerifyState = { status:"idle" } | { status:"verifying" } | { status:"done"; result: VerifyRunResponse } | { status:"error"; message:string }
- state + `const verifyInFlightRef = useRef(false);`
- In handleSelectRecord: reset `setVerifyState({status:"idle"}); verifyInFlightRef.current = false;` before fetching.
- Handler:

```ts
async function handleVerifyRecord() {
  if (selectedRecord === null) return;
  if (verifyInFlightRef.current) return;   // 连续点击只发一次
  verifyInFlightRef.current = true;
  const token = selectedFetchTokenRef.current; // 绑定当前选中
  setVerifyState({ status: "verifying" });
  try {
    const result = await verifyRun(selectedRecord.id);
    if (token !== selectedFetchTokenRef.current) return; // 切换了记录，丢弃旧响应
    setVerifyState({ status: "done", result });
  } catch (error) {
    if (token !== selectedFetchTokenRef.current) return;
    setVerifyState({ status: "error", message: errorMessage(error) });
  } finally {
    if (token === selectedFetchTokenRef.current) {
      verifyInFlightRef.current = false;
    }
  }
}
```

JSX text: "记录版本 v" + expr + " · 当前版本\n                        v" + expr. The raw text between `当前版本` and `v` is newline + spaces → JSX collapses whitespace including newlines into a single space. So "当前版本 v1". Good.

But Testing Library's getByText normalizes whitespace by default for string matcher; regex against textContent normalized? `getByText(/.../)` checks normalized text. Should be fine.

## 一、后端：POST /api/runs/:id/verify

新增只读核对模块 [verify.ts](file:///D:/ago/link/apps/api/src/verify.ts)：

```ts
export function verifyRunRecord(scenario: Scenario, record: RunRecord): VerifyRunResponse {
  const versions = {
    recordScenarioId: record.scenarioId,
    recordScenarioVersion: record.scenarioVersion,
    currentScenarioId: scenario.id,
    currentScenarioVersion: scenario.version
  };

  if (record.scenarioId !== scenario.id ||
      record.scenarioVersion !== scenario.version) {
    return { status: "version-mismatch", ...versions };   // 不调用 evaluateRun
  }

  const recomputed = evaluateRun(scenario, record.input);

  if (!isDeepStrictEqual(recomputed, record.result)) {
    return { status: "result-mismatch", ...versions,
             differences: summarizeDifferences(record.result, recomputed) };
  }

  return { status: "match", ...versions };
}
```

路由 [app.ts](file:///D:/ago/link/apps/api/src/app.ts#L212-L237)：`runStore.findById` 不存在返回 404「运行记录不存在」，否则返回核对结果。**零副作用**：只读 `findById`，不写 runs、不重新生成解释、不触碰 learning_records。

新增协议 [contracts/verify.ts](file:///D:/ago/link/packages/contracts/src/verify.ts)：`VerifyStatus`（三状态）与 `VerifyRunResponse`（含两侧场景 ID/版本、可选 `differences`）。[index.ts](file:///D:/ago/link/packages/contracts/src/index.ts#L138) 仅追加 1 行导出，删除行数 0。

差异摘要只在 `result-mismatch` 时生成：事件数量、第一条差异事件、路径结论、告警数量，不写库。

## 二、测试（[verify.test.ts](file:///D:/ago/link/apps/api/tests/verify.test.ts) 6 项）

1. match：GET 原文 before → verify → after 完全相等；同版本确实调用引擎 1 次。
2. 核对不改 `input_json` / `result_json` / `explanation` 三字段原文（直接 `DatabaseSync` 读测试库比对），且已有 learning_records 行原文不变、总数不变。
3. version-mismatch（版本 99）：`isDeepStrictEqual` 前置分支直接返回，`vi.mock("@link-lab/engine", { spy: true })` 断言引擎调用次数不变；GET 原文与库内原文不变。
4. scenarioId 不一致：同样 version-mismatch 且不重算。
5. result-mismatch：同版本但告警数改写的伪造快照 → 返回差异摘要，旧快照仍原文可读，且未写 learning_records。
6. 不存在的 id → 404。

实际输出：`apps/api` 6 个文件 **48 项全部通过**（含原有 app/runs/list-runs/learning/explanation 无回归）。
